# Wajik Anime API — JSON Respons Lengkap

Audit HTTP lokal. Setiap blok di bawah memuat body JSON aktual lengkap untuk request yang dicantumkan; array tidak dipotong. Respons provider bersifat dinamis. Endpoint batch Kuramanime dapat mengembalikan 404 jika tidak ada batch untuk anime sampel.

Total route-method source: 32. Request tersimpan: 43.
Format respons: HTTP status lalu body JSON mentah yang diterima dari server lokal.

## Global

### GET / — Katalog endpoint global

HTTP status: 200

```json
{
  "statusCode": 200,
  "statusMessage": "OK",
  "message": "",
  "data": {
    "routes": [
      {
        "method": "GET",
        "path": "/otakudesu",
        "description": "Otakudesu",
        "pathParams": [],
        "queryParams": []
      },
      {
        "method": "GET",
        "path": "/kuramanime",
        "description": "Kuramanime",
        "pathParams": [],
        "queryParams": []
      },
      {
        "method": "GET",
        "path": "/oploverz",
        "description": "Oploverz",
        "pathParams": [],
        "queryParams": []
      }
    ]
  },
  "pagination": null
}
```
